- **sources-critic**: Contradiction finder. Scans an evidence corpus for statements that cannot all be true — a rule stated two incompatible ways, disagreeing numbers, a decision that violates a stated constraint, mutually exclusive requirements, a silent reversal. Returns each conflict with its clashing statements quoted verbatim and anchored by source + loc, plus a clarifying question for the caller to put to the user. Detects and asks; never resolves, never edits the corpus. Writes the report and a machine-readable summary. Invoked with the corpus path. ([DomainDrivers/dd-ai](https://github.com/DomainDrivers/dd-ai/blob/HEAD/002/.claude/agents/sources-critic.md) · ★9)

- **sous-chef**: Use this agent when a logical chunk of code has been written or modified and needs review before proceeding. This includes after implementing a feature, fixing a bug, refactoring code, or completing any task that involves code changes. The agent should be called proactively after significant code changes to catch issues early. Examples: &lt;example&gt; Context: User asked to implement a new feature and the code has been written. user: "Add a function to validate email addresses" assistant: "Here is the email validation function I've implemented:" &lt;function implementation completed&gt; assistant: "Now… ([smileynet/line-cook](https://github.com/smileynet/line-cook/blob/HEAD/plugins/claude-code/agents/sous-chef.md) · ★10)
- **sous-chef**: Use this agent when reviewing Java/Spring Boot code, checking best practices, validating test coverage, or ensuring code quality. Trigger automatically when user says "review [file]", "check [code]", "before committing", or "I just finished implementing [Service|Controller|Repository]". Examples - User: "Review my PokService implementation" → Use this agent. User: "Check this endpoint for best practices" → Use this agent. ([lucasxf/engineering-daybook](https://github.com/lucasxf/engineering-daybook/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sous-chef.md) · ★3)
- **sous-chef**: Senior specialist for advanced development, hard debugging and difficult research. Use for multi-file features, intermittent or unexplained bugs, performance work, migrations and research that needs judgment. Not for simple lookups. ([Velavalapallisairam/Auto-Router](https://github.com/Velavalapallisairam/Auto-Router/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-chef.md) · ★1)
- **sous-chef**: Sous-Chef — the second palate. Reviews the chef's artifact (strategy doc, implementation plan, or workflow script) for soundness, testability, coverage, and risk before the brigade fires it. Critiques; never rewrites. Lean, stack-agnostic equivalent of the muppets' statler. ([avegancafe/kitchen-brigade](https://github.com/avegancafe/kitchen-brigade/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-chef.md))
- **sous-chef**: Opus at high effort for work where mistakes are costly - UI with design judgment, auth/payments/Firebase rules, hard-to-find bugs, code review, changes to live/production projects. - **sous-explorer**: sous agent. Never edits anything. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-explorer.md))
- **sous-intake**: sous agent. Parses one Qonflo ticket before the planner - reads the Jira ticket (custom fields, comments), its epic, remote links and the contract search of rules/ticket-contract.md, and writes ac.txt, materials/jira.md and materials/contract.md to the ticket's blackboard. Never edits code. Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-intake.md))
- **sous-jira**: sous agent. Creates QRD Jira tickets from drafts written on a board, and edits only tickets it created earlier. Never comments, transitions, assigns or touches other tickets. Only use when a sous runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-jira.md))
- **sous-light-runner**: sous agent. Runs one small fix on a Qonflo ticket without the full pipeline - scout gate, brief, worker, verify, optional browser check, blast check, publish - by dispatching the existing sous-* agents, keeping the ledger at mode=light, and hands the ticket back to the full runner when the gate fails. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a light run. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-light-runner.md))
- **sous-planner**: sous agent. Plays S0 for one Qonflo ticket - reads the AC and materials sous-intake wrote to the ticket's blackboard and the code, maps the terrain, and writes the session prompt prompts/S&lt;n&gt;.md. Never edits code. Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-planner.md))
- **sous-pr-reviewer**: sous agent. Reviews a teammate's Qonflo pull request following the review-pr skill - contract first, AC to path:line, blast radius, screenshots for frontend work - and returns findings with severities and a verdict. Never edits code, never comments on the PR. Only use when sous-review-runner dispatches it. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-pr-reviewer.md))
- **sous-publisher**: sous agent. Stages a reviewed and verified Qonflo change per path, commits it, pushes its feat|fix/&lt;KEY&gt;-... branch and opens the pull request (draft when told). Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches a publish. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-publisher.md))
- **sous-review-runner**: sous agent. Runs one review of a teammate's Qonflo PR end to end - PR state, ledger kind=review, sous-pr-reviewer, verdict in the ledger - and returns a short verdict summary. Never comments on the PR, never approves it. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a review. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-review-runner.md))
- **sous-reviewer**: sous agent. Reviews a Qonflo ticket's change against its acceptance criteria - LOOP on one task before verification (sonnet), FINAL on the whole ticket with blast radius before publishing (runner passes model opus). Every AC line needs a path:line; FINAL findings name the task they belong to. Never edits code. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-reviewer.md))
- **sous-ticket-runner**: sous agent. Runs one Qonflo ticket end to end - intake, terrain, worktrees, tasks (worker, review loop, verify), final review, video, publish - by dispatching the other sous-* agents, keeping the ledger current with status.cjs, and returns a short summary. Only use when the sous main session dispatches a ticket. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-ticket-runner.md))
- **sous-ticket-worker**: sous agent. Implements one task of a Qonflo ticket from its blackboard session prompt inside the ticket's prepared worktree. Writes code only; never runs tests, git or installs. Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches a task. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-ticket-worker.md))
- **sous-verifier**: sous agent. Runs the related tests, TEST-CMD lines and type checks of one task on a prepared Qonflo worktree while the runner holds `status.cjs lock verify`, then reports. Never edits code, never reads Jira, never runs git writes. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-verifier.md))
- **sous-video**: sous agent. Makes the narrated demo video of one Qonflo ticket by following the demo-video skill end to end - Indonesian narration from the AC, ElevenLabs voice, headless recording of the local app, Remotion render - and returns the render path and its length. Never runs git or gh. Only use when sous-ticket-runner dispatches the video. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-video.md))
- **sous-work-runner**: sous agent. Runs one free-form piece of work (a DOCK card, ledger key CARD-&lt;slug&gt;) - intake, steps through sous-analyst and sous-verifier, card upkeep - and returns a short summary. Never sends, shares or contacts anyone. Only use when the sous main session dispatches free-form work. ([danuseta/claude-config](https://github.com/danuseta/claude-config/blob/HEAD/agents/sous-work-runner.md))

- **sov**: Main Soveraeign operating profile. The one participant that may plan, build, and land a concern in the same pass: it holds no tool restriction and no commit prohibition, and it is the actor named in the repository's only ratified grant. Use it to carry a bounded concern from selection to a landed result. It may never witness its own work, so a landing always needs a second participant. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov.md))
- **sov-comms**: Communications tier. Two jobs: answer Bdo in English when launched to talk to him, and carry work across a concern or domain boundary so the receiving side gets what the sending side actually established. It builds nothing, witnesses nothing, settles nothing, and holds no grant. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov-comms.md))
- **sov-controller**: Stable Control-tier role for headless or scheduled duty. Use it to select and dispatch domain work, aggregate reports, maintain Bdo's judgement queue, and produce a completion report. It does not build, plan a single-domain operation, or witness claims. Bdo or an interactive host launches it explicitly; the interactive session does not hold this role by default. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov-controller.md))
- **sov-orchestrator**: Stable Orchestration-tier role for any Soveraeign domain. Use it to turn a named objective into bounded operations, files, effect classes, dependencies, observations, and blockers. It plans and sequences; after work, REVIEW mode may evaluate how the participant carried the assignment. It does not build, witness the work, settle, or dispatch workflows. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov-orchestrator.md))
- **sov-witness**: Independent witness for Soveraeign domain work. Use it after a work subject is ready to verify observable claims through an independent path, run repository and conformance checks, inspect changes against contracts and fixtures, and emit an attributable observation. It never builds, edits, fixes, settles, or ratifies. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov-witness.md))
- **sov-worker**: Stable Work-tier builder for any Soveraeign domain. Use it to execute exactly one bounded, planned operation. Domain scope, blockers, boundaries, and checks come from the matching sov-&lt;domain&gt; skill. Do not use it to plan multi-step work, witness claims, or dispatch workflows. ([bdf1992/Soveraeign](https://github.com/bdf1992/Soveraeign/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/sov-worker.md))
